Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any isotopes module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 720. The projective resolution has length at most 26 with Betti numbers bounded by 2.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to isotopes theory, we decompose the problem into 720 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 26. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(2/n). QED.
